> AGENT

Here's a detailed breakdown of every card in your Trello **"To Do"** list. I've read both screenshots, de-duplicated the overlapping cards, grouped them by area, and added a **difficulty colour badge** (easy → hard) based on how much work each one likely needs.

### Difficulty legend
| Badge | Meaning |
|---|---|
| 🟢 **Easy** | Label/text tweak, hide-or-show, small cosmetic fix |
| 🟡 **Medium** | Form fields, filters, re-ordering, moderate UI + a bit of logic |
| 🟠 **Hard** | New flow or screen, status-machine change, multi-step UX |
| 🔴 **Complex** | New data fields end-to-end (DB → API → UI) or structural rethink |

---

### A. Order / Payment details (Farmer side)
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Payment shown as **"UPI – Pending"**, should be only **"UPI"** | On the farmer's order-details view the payment method displays the status suffix too. Strip the "– Pending" and show just the method name (`UPI`). | 🟢 Easy |
| 2 | Order details should show **self-pickup date** | When the order is a self-pickup, the chosen pickup date isn't displayed. Add it to the farmer's order-detail screen. | 🟢 Easy |
| 3 | Progress picture should show the **date of each status** | The order status/progress tracker shows the steps but not *when* each step happened. Add a timestamp/date under each status node. | 🟡 Medium |
| 5 | **"Shipped" status missing** | After the farmer confirms the shipping date, the next action button jumps straight to **Delivered**. It should go **Confirm date → Shipped → Delivered**. This is a status-machine fix. | 🟠 Hard |

---

### B. Produce profile & search
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 4 | **Produce review missing** in Produce tab of farmer profile | Reviews exist elsewhere but don't appear under the Produce tab on the farmer profile. Surface them there. | 🟡 Medium |
| 8 | Search shows only **3 farming methods**, but farmer can pick **4** | When adding produce a farmer can choose 4 methods (incl. **Chemical**), but the search filter only offers 3. Add the 4th. Also **rename "Low Chemical" → "Semi Organic"** everywhere. | 🟡 Medium |
| 9 | Category filter (Veg/Fruits/Grains&Pulses/Leafy Greens) has **no matching field when adding produce** | Produce is filtered by category on the buyer side, but the "add produce" form has no field to set that category — so filtering can't work reliably. Add a category selector to the form. | 🟡 Medium |
| 12 | Allow **multiple photos** per produce | Farmer should be able to upload more than one photo per produce, and consumers should be able to view/scroll them (gallery). Needs upload + storage + carousel UI. | 🟠 Hard |

---

### C. Consumer side
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 6 | **Raise an Intent** — why WhatsApp & delivery location? | Question/cleanup: the "Raise an Intent" form asks for WhatsApp and delivery location — review whether those fields are actually needed and trim them. | 🟢 Easy |
| 7 | Consumer should **see / edit / remove their raised intents** | Buyers currently can't manage intents they've raised. Add a "My Intents" list with edit + delete. New CRUD flow. | 🟠 Hard |
| 10 | Remove the **duplicate "My Orders"** on consumer page | There are two "My Orders" entries; delete one. | 🟢 Easy |

---

### D. Farmer profile layout & schedule-visit
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 11 | Move **"Schedule a Visit"** next to farmer's name (above Follow) | Reposition the Schedule-a-Visit action to sit beside the farmer name, above the Follow button. | 🟢 Easy |

---

### E. Farmer dashboard / thumbnails (the stat tiles)
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 13 | Possibly **remove "Your Produce" section** | Produce management isn't a daily task; a managed list already exists in the thumbnails. Consider removing the standalone section (order management stays). Decision + cleanup. | 🟡 Medium |
| 14 | **"Approved" thumbnail** should open Orders list | Tapping the Approved tile should deep-link into the Orders list pre-filtered to *Approved, all-time*. | 🟡 Medium |
| 15 | Remove **"Today's schedule"** section | Redundant — "Today's orders" can be launched from the thumbnail tile instead. Remove the separate section. | 🟢 Easy |
| 16 | **Re-order the thumbnails** | Fixed order requested: **1) Today's Orders, 2) Active Listings, 3) Approved this week, 4) Revenue.** | 🟢 Easy |

---

### F. Farmer profile — missing data fields
| # | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|
| 17 | Profile **missing details**: WhatsApp, Farm Size, Soil Organic Carbon, Story/Quote | These fields aren't shown on the farmer-module profile. Need to capture + display them. | 🔴 Complex |
| 18 | Where is **"Soil Health / How we grow"** info entered? | Kapil Konedena's profile shows this info — trace where it's authored/stored so it can be edited consistently. Investigation + wiring. | 🟡 Medium |
| 19 | **Soil pH missing** from farmer profile | Soil pH shows on the public Farmer Profile but is absent in the farmer module. Add the field through the stack. | 🔴 Complex |
| 20 | **Chemicals & Buyers showing zero** | These counters read 0 — figure out the data source and connect them so real values appear. | 🔴 Complex |

---

### Quick priority read
- **Fastest wins (🟢):** #1, #2, #6, #10, #11, #15, #16 — mostly label/layout fixes you could knock out in one sitting.
- **Solid middle (🟡):** #3, #4, #8, #9, #13, #14, #18.
- **Real features (🟠):** #5, #7, #12 — flows/status changes.
- **End-to-end data work (🔴):** #17, #19, #20 — new fields/counters that touch DB → API → UI.

### Difficulty legend
🟢 **Easy** = label/layout tweak · 🟡 **Medium** = fields/filters/moderate logic · 🟠 **Hard** = new flow/status change · 🔴 **Complex** = end-to-end data field/counter

| # | Area | Card | What it actually means | Difficulty |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Order/Payment | Payment shown as **"UPI – Pending"** | Should display only `UPI` on farmer's order details — strip the status suffix. | 🟢 Easy |
| 2 | Order/Payment | Show **self-pickup date** | Add the chosen pickup date to the farmer's order-detail screen. | 🟢 Easy |
| 3 | Order/Payment | **Date for each status** in progress | Progress tracker shows steps but not *when* — add a timestamp under each status node. | 🟡 Medium |
| 4 | Produce | **Produce review missing** in Produce tab | Reviews don't show under the Produce tab on farmer profile — surface them there. | 🟡 Medium |
| 5 | Order/Payment | **"Shipped" status missing** | After confirming ship date the next button jumps to Delivered; insert **Shipped** between (status-machine fix). | 🟠 Hard |
| 6 | Consumer | **Raise an Intent** — why WhatsApp/location? | Review whether WhatsApp + delivery-location fields are needed; trim them. | 🟢 Easy |
| 7 | Consumer | **See/edit/remove raised intents** | Add a "My Intents" list with edit + delete (new CRUD flow). | 🟠 Hard |
| 8 | Produce/Search | Search has **3 methods**, farmer picks **4** | Add the 4th (**Chemical**) to search filter; also rename **"Low Chemical" → "Semi Organic"** everywhere. | 🟡 Medium |
| 9 | Produce/Search | **Category field missing** when adding produce | Buyers filter by Veg/Fruits/Grains&Pulses/Leafy Greens, but add-produce form has no category selector — add it. | 🟡 Medium |
| 10 | Consumer | Remove **duplicate "My Orders"** | Two "My Orders" on consumer page — delete one. | 🟢 Easy |
| 11 | Farmer profile | Move **"Schedule a Visit"** | Place it next to the farmer's name, above the Follow button. | 🟢 Easy |
| 12 | Produce | Allow **multiple photos** per produce | Farmer uploads several photos; consumer views them in a gallery (upload + storage + carousel). | 🟠 Hard |
| 13 | Dashboard | Possibly **remove "Your Produce"** section | Managed list already in thumbnails; produce mgmt isn't daily — decide + remove section. | 🟡 Medium |
| 14 | Dashboard | **"Approved" thumbnail** opens Orders | Tile should deep-link to Orders list filtered to *Approved, all-time*. | 🟡 Medium |
| 15 | Dashboard | Remove **"Today's schedule"** section | Redundant — launch Today's orders from the thumbnail tile instead. | 🟢 Easy |
| 16 | Dashboard | **Re-order thumbnails** | Order: 1) Today's Orders, 2) Active Listings, 3) Approved this week, 4) Revenue. | 🟢 Easy |
| 17 | Farmer profile | **Missing fields**: WhatsApp, Farm Size, Soil Organic Carbon, Story/Quote | Capture + display these in the farmer module (DB → API → UI). | 🔴 Complex |
| 18 | Farmer profile | Where is **"Soil Health / How we grow"** entered? | Trace where this info (shown on Kapil's profile) is authored/stored so it's editable. | 🟡 Medium |
| 19 | Farmer profile | **Soil pH missing** | Shows on public profile but absent in farmer module — add field through the stack. | 🔴 Complex |
| 20 | Farmer profile | **Chemicals & Buyers show zero** | Find the data source and wire the counters to real values. | 🔴 Complex |

**Tally:** 🟢 Easy ×7 · 🟡 Medium ×7 · 🟠 Hard ×3 · 🔴 Complex ×3

```
1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
3	
4	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
5	import { Suspense } from 'react'
6	import TopNav from '@/components/farmer/TopNav'
7	import FarmCover from '@/components/farmer/FarmCover'
8	import TrustStrip from '@/components/farmer/TrustStrip'
9	import TabSection from '@/components/farmer/TabSection'
10	import { CartFab } from '@/components/consumer/Cart'
11	
12	export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
13	  const { slug } = await params
14	
15	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
16	    .from('farmers')
17	    .select('id, name, village, district, story_quote')
18	    .eq('slug', slug)
19	    .single()
20	
21	  if (!farmer) return { title: 'Farmer not found' }
22	
23	  const { data: firstPhoto } = await supabase
24	    .from('media')
25	    .select('url')
26	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
27	    .eq('type', 'photo')
28	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
29	    .limit(1)
30	    .single()
31	
32	  const title = `${farmer.name} — Natural farmer in ${farmer.village}`
33	  const description =
34	    farmer.story_quote ??
35	    `Buy natural produce directly from ${farmer.name}, ${farmer.village}, ${farmer.district}. No middlemen. No chemicals.`
36	
37	  return {
38	    title: `${farmer.name}'s Farm — YourFamilyFarmer`,
39	    description,
40	    openGraph: {
41	      title,
42	      description,
43	      siteName: 'YourFamilyFarmer',
44	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [{ url: firstPhoto.url, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: `${farmer.name}'s farm` }] } : {}),
45	    },
46	    twitter: {
47	      card: 'summary_large_image',
48	      title,
49	      description,
50	      ...(firstPhoto?.url ? { images: [firstPhoto.url] } : {}),
51	    },
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	export default async function FarmerPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
56	  const { slug } = await params
57	
58	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
59	    .from('farmers')
60	    .select('*')
61	    .eq('slug', slug)
62	    .eq('active', true)
63	    .single()
64	
65	  if (!farmer) notFound()
66	
67	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
68	    .from('produce_listings')
69	    .select('*')
70	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
71	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
72	
73	  const { data: reviews } = await supabase
74	    .from('reviews')
75	    .select('*')
76	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
77	    .eq('approved', true)
78	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
79	
80	  // Verified per-order buyer feedback (the ⭐ button consumers tap on their
81	  // orders) lives in produce_reviews, tied to a listing. Only feedback on a
82	  // completed order is approved/public. Surface it on the farmer's profile too —
83	  // tagged with the produce it was about — so consumer feedback isn't invisible
84	  // here, and fold it into the same Reviews tab + star average as written reviews.
85	  const { data: produceReviews } = await supabase
86	    .from('produce_reviews')
87	    .select('id, reviewer_name, star_rating, review_text, created_at, produce_listing_id')
88	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
89	    .eq('approved', true)
90	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
91	
92	  // Resolve the produce names with a separate query rather than a PostgREST
93	  // embedded join (produce_reviews → produce_listings). That relationship isn't
94	  // in the schema cache, so embedding it made the whole produce_reviews query
95	  // error out and return null — which silently hid ALL verified buyer feedback
96	  // from the profile (showing "No reviews yet" and a 0 rating even when approved
97	  // reviews existed).
98	  const listingIds = [
99	    ...new Set((produceReviews ?? []).map((r) => r.produce_listing_id).filter(Boolean) as string[]),
100	  ]
101	  const { data: listingNames } = listingIds.length
102	    ? await supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id, name').in('id', listingIds)
103	    : { data: [] as { id: string; name: string }[] }
104	  const nameById = new Map((listingNames ?? []).map((l) => [l.id, l.name]))
105	
106	  const mappedProduceReviews = (produceReviews ?? []).map((r) => ({
107	    id: r.id,
108	    reviewer_name: r.reviewer_name,
109	    star_rating: r.star_rating,
110	    review_text: r.review_text,
111	    produce_ordered: r.produce_listing_id ? nameById.get(r.produce_listing_id) ?? null : null,
112	    created_at: r.created_at,
113	  }))
114	
115	  // Both sources, newest first — one combined list for the Reviews tab.
116	  const allReviews = [...(reviews ?? []), ...mappedProduceReviews].sort(
117	    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at as string).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at as string).getTime(),
118	  )
119	
120	  // Star rating shown in the trust strip reflects all real feedback (written
121	  // reviews + verified order feedback). Falls back to the stored value if there
122	  // are no reviews yet.
123	  const ratingAvg =
124	    allReviews.length > 0
125	      ? Math.round(
126	          (allReviews.reduce((sum, r) => sum + ((r.star_rating as number) ?? 0), 0) / allReviews.length) * 10,
127	        ) / 10
128	      : (farmer.rating_avg ?? 0)
129	
130	  const { data: media } = await supabase
131	    .from('media')
132	    .select('*')
133	    .eq('farmer_id', farmer.id)
134	    .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
135	
136	  return (
137	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-24">
138	      <TopNav regionSlug={farmer.region_slug} />
139	      <FarmCover farmer={farmer} />
140	      {/* Count only live listings — a produce the farmer has suspended (or that
141	          is still "coming soon") must not inflate the public "Produce now" stat. */}
142	      <TrustStrip
143	        farmer={{ ...farmer, rating_avg: ratingAvg }}
144	        produceCount={(produce ?? []).filter((p) => (p as { status?: string }).status === 'available').length}
145	      />
146	      <Suspense fallback={null}>
147	        <TabSection
148	          farmer={farmer}
149	          produce={produce ?? []}
150	          reviews={allReviews}
151	          media={media ?? []}
152	        />
153	      </Suspense>
154	      <CartFab />
155	    </main>
156	  )
157	}
158	
```

```
28	  shelf_life_days?: number
29	  available_to?: string
30	  description?: string
31	  image_url?: string
32	  harvest_date?: string | null
33	  delivery_mode?: string | null
34	  delivery_charge?: number | null
35	  delivery_radius_km?: number | null
36	}
37	
38	type Farmer = {
39	  id?: string
40	  name?: string
41	  phone?: string
42	  village?: string
43	  slug?: string
44	  pickup_locations?: string[] | null
45	  pickup_slots?: unknown
46	  upi_id?: string | null
47	}
48	
49	const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾']
50	const STATUS_OPTIONS = ['available', 'coming_soon']
51	
52	export default function ProduceTab({
53	  farmer,
54	  produce,
55	  isEditMode = false,
56	}: {
57	  farmer: Record<string, unknown>
58	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
59	  isEditMode?: boolean
60	}) {
61	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
62	  const f = farmer as Farmer
63	  const { addItem } = useCart()
64	  const { requireAuth } = useConsumerAuth()
65	  const [listings, setListings] = useState<Produce[]>(produce as Produce[])
66	  const available = listings.filter((p) => p.status === 'available' && p.stock_qty !== 0)
67	  const comingSoon = listings.filter((p) => p.status === 'coming_soon')
68	
69	  const handleProduceAdded = (newItem: Produce) => {
70	    setListings((prev) => [newItem, ...prev])
71	  }
72	
73	  const handleDelete = async (id: string) => {
74	    if (!confirm(L('Are you sure?', 'మీరు నిశ్చితంగా ఉన్నారా?'))) return
75	    const { error } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').delete().eq('id', id)
76	    if (error) {
77	      alert(`Could not delete: ${error.message}`)
78	      return
79	    }
80	    setListings((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id))
81	  }
82	
83	  const farmerCartInfo = {
84	    farmerId: f.id ?? '',
85	    farmerName: f.name ?? '',
86	    farmerPhone: f.phone ?? '',
87	    farmerVillage: f.village ?? '',
88	    farmerSlug: f.slug ?? '',
89	    farmerPickupLocations: Array.isArray(f.pickup_locations) ? f.pickup_locations : [],
90	    farmerPickupSlots: normalizePickupSchedule(f.pickup_slots, Array.isArray(f.pickup_locations) ? f.pickup_locations : []),
91	    farmerUpiId: f.upi_id ?? undefined,
92	  }
93	
94	  return (
95	    <div className="space-y-6">
96	      {available.length > 0 && (
97	        <div>
98	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
99	            <span className="bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
100	              {tx.availableNow}
101	            </span>
102	          </div>
103	          <div className="space-y-3">
104	            {available.map((item) => (
105	              <ProduceCard
106	                key={item.id}
107	                item={item}
108	                farmerInfo={farmerCartInfo}
109	                onAddToCart={(qty) => requireAuth(() => addItem({
110	                  listingId: item.id,
111	                  name: item.name,
112	                  variety: item.variety,
113	                  emoji: item.emoji,
114	                  pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
115	                  priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
116	                  priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
117	                  priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
118	                  priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
119	                  priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
120	                  unit: undefined,
121	                  stockQty: item.stock_qty,
122	                  ...farmerCartInfo,
123	                }, qty))}
124	                onDelete={isEditMode ? () => handleDelete(item.id) : undefined}
125	              />
126	            ))}
127	          </div>
128	        </div>
129	      )}
130	
131	      {comingSoon.length > 0 && (
132	        <div>
133	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
134	            <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
135	              {tx.comingSoon}
136	            </span>
137	          </div>
138	          <div className="space-y-3">
139	            {comingSoon.map((item) => (
140	              <ComingSoonCard
141	                key={item.id}
142	                item={item}
143	                farmerId={item.id}
144	                onDelete={isEditMode ? () => handleDelete(item.id) : undefined}
145	              />
146	            ))}
147	          </div>
148	        </div>
149	      )}
150	
151	      {listings.length === 0 && (
152	        <div className="text-center py-6 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noProduceListed}</div>
153	      )}
154	
155	      {isEditMode && f.id && (
156	        <AddProduceForm farmerId={f.id} onAdded={handleProduceAdded} />
157	      )}
158	    </div>
159	  )
160	}
161	
162	function AddProduceForm({ farmerId, onAdded }: { farmerId: string; onAdded: (item: Produce) => void }) {
163	  const { L } = useLang()
164	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
165	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
166	  const [variety, setVariety] = useState('')
167	  const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('🌿')
168	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'available' | 'coming_soon'>('available')
169	  const [price, setPrice] = useState('')
170	  const [stock, setStock] = useState('')
171	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
172	  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
173	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
174	
175	  const reset = () => {
176	    setName(''); setVariety(''); setEmoji('🌿'); setStatus('available')
177	    setPrice(''); setStock(''); setSuccess(false); setError('')
178	  }
179	
180	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
181	    if (!name.trim()) return
182	    setLoading(true)
183	    setError('')
184	
185	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
186	      farmer_id: farmerId,
187	      name: name.trim(),
188	      emoji,
189	      status,
190	      method: 'Natural',
191	    }
192	    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
193	    if (price) payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price)
194	    if (stock) payload.stock_qty = Number(stock)
195	
196	    const { data, error: insertError } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').insert(payload).select().single()
197	    setLoading(false)
198	    if (!insertError && data) {
199	      onAdded(data as Produce)
200	      setSuccess(true)
201	      setTimeout(() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }, 1500)
202	    } else {
203	      setError(insertError?.message ?? 'Failed to add produce. Check Supabase RLS policies.')
204	    }
205	  }
206	
207	  if (!open) {
208	    return (
209	      <button
210	        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
211	        className="w-full border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 text-green-700 text-sm font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:border-green-500 hover:bg-green-50 transition-colors"
212	      >
213	        <span className="text-lg">+</span> {L('Add your produce', 'మీ పంట చేర్చండి')}
214	      </button>
215	    )
216	  }
217	
218	  return (
219	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-4 space-y-4">
220	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
221	        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{L('Add produce', 'పంట చేర్చండి')}</h3>
222	        <button onClick={() => { reset(); setOpen(false) }} className="text-gray-400 text-lg leading-none">×</button>
223	      </div>
224	
225	      {/* Emoji picker */}
226	      <div>
227	        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">{L('Pick an icon', 'ఐకాన్ ఎంచుకోండి')}</p>
228	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
229	          {EMOJI_OPTIONS.map((e) => (
230	            <button
231	              key={e}
232	              onClick={() => setEmoji(e)}
233	              className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg text-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${emoji === e ? 'bg-green-100 ring-2 ring-green-500' : 'bg-gray-50'}`}
234	            >
235	              {e}
236	            </button>
237	          ))}
238	        </div>
239	      </div>
240	
241	      {/* Name + variety */}
242	      <div className="space-y-2">
243	        <input
244	          type="text"
245	          placeholder={L('Produce name', 'పంట పేరు (e.g. Papaya)')}
246	          value={name}
247	          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
248	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
249	        />
250	        <input
251	          type="text"
252	          placeholder={L('Variety (optional)', 'రకం')}
253	          value={variety}
254	          onChange={(e) => setVariety(e.target.value)}
255	          className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
256	        />
257	      </div>
258	
259	      {/* Price + stock */}
260	      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
261	        <input
262	          type="number"
263	          placeholder={L('Price / kg (₹)', 'ధర / కేజీ (₹)')}
264	          value={price}
265	          onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)}
266	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
267	        />
268	        <input
269	          type="number"
270	          placeholder={L('Stock (kg)', 'నిల్వ')}
271	          value={stock}
272	          onChange={(e) => setStock(e.target.value)}
273	          className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm"
274	        />
275	      </div>
276	
277	      {/* Status */}
278	      <div className="flex gap-2">
279	        {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((s) => (
280	          <button
281	            key={s}
282	            onClick={() => setStatus(s as 'available' | 'coming_soon')}
283	            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
284	              status === s
285	                ? s === 'available' ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-amber-500 text-white'
286	                : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'
287	            }`}
288	          >
289	            {s === 'available' ? L('Available now', 'అందుబాటులో') : L('Coming soon', 'త్వరలో')}
290	          </button>
291	        ))}
292	      </div>
293	
294	      {error && (
295	        <p className="text-center text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
296	      )}
297	
298	      {success ? (
299	        <p className="text-center text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold py-1">
300	          {L('✓ Added successfully!', 'చేర్చబడింది!')}
301	        </p>
302	      ) : (
303	        <button
304	          onClick={handleSubmit}
305	          disabled={loading || !name.trim()}
306	          className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50"
307	        >
308	          {loading ? L('Adding...', 'చేరుస్తోంది') : L('Add produce', 'పంట చేర్చండి')}
309	        </button>
310	      )}
311	    </div>
312	  )
313	}
314	
315	type FarmerCartInfo = {
316	  farmerId: string
317	  farmerName: string
318	  farmerPhone: string
319	  farmerVillage: string
320	  farmerSlug: string
321	  farmerPickupLocations: string[]
322	  farmerUpiId?: string
323	}

325	function ProduceCard({
326	  item,
327	  farmerInfo,
328	  onAddToCart,
329	  onDelete,
330	}: {
331	  item: Produce
332	  farmerInfo: FarmerCartInfo
333	  onAddToCart: (qty: number) => void
334	  onDelete?: () => void
335	}) {
336	  const { tx, lang, L } = useLang()
337	  const [added, setAdded] = useState(false)
338	
339	  const bgColors: Record<string, string> = {
340	    '🍅': 'bg-red-100', '🥬': 'bg-green-100', '🥭': 'bg-orange-100',
341	    '🍆': 'bg-purple-100', '🥕': 'bg-orange-100', '🌽': 'bg-yellow-100',
342	    '🍌': 'bg-yellow-100', '🫑': 'bg-green-100',
343	  }
344	  const emoji = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
345	  const bg = bgColors[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-100'
346	
347	  const handleAdd = () => {
348	    onAddToCart(1)
349	    setAdded(true)
350	    setTimeout(() => setAdded(false), 1500)
351	  }
352	
353	  return (
354	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
355	      <div className="flex gap-3 p-3">
356	        {item.image_url ? (
357	          <img
358	            src={item.image_url}
359	            alt={item.name}
360	            loading="lazy"
361	            className="rounded-xl w-14 h-14 object-cover flex-shrink-0 bg-gray-100"
362	          />
363	        ) : (
364	          <div className={`${bg} rounded-xl w-14 h-14 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl flex-shrink-0`}>
365	            {emoji}
366	          </div>
367	        )}
368	        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
369	          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
370	            <div>
371	              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-sm">{localizeName(item.name, lang)}</h3>
372	              {item.variety && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{localizeName(item.variety, lang)}</p>}
373	            </div>
374	            {item.stock_qty !== undefined && (
375	              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex-shrink-0">{item.stock_qty} {tx.stockLeft}</span>
376	            )}
377	          </div>
378	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1.5">
379	            {item.method && (
380	              <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{item.method}</span>
381	            )}
382	            {item.pesticide_result && (
383	              <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{item.pesticide_result}</span>
384	            )}
385	            {item.brix && (
386	              <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">BRIX {item.brix}</span>
387	            )}
388	            {item.harvest_date && <FreshnessBadge harvestDate={item.harvest_date} />}
389	            {(item.delivery_mode === 'courier' || item.delivery_mode === 'both') && (
390	              <span className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
391	                🛵 {(item.delivery_charge ?? 0) > 0 ? `Delivery ₹${item.delivery_charge}` : 'Free delivery'}
392	                {item.delivery_radius_km ? ` · ${item.delivery_radius_km}km` : ''}
393	              </span>
394	            )}
395	          </div>
396	          {item.description && (
397	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-2 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line">
398	              {item.description}
399	            </p>
400	          )}
401	        </div>
402	      </div>
403	
404	      {item.price_tier_1_price && (
405	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-50">
406	          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-700">
407	            {item.price_tier_1_price && (
408	              <span>{item.price_tier_1_qty ? `1–${item.price_tier_2_qty ? item.price_tier_2_qty - 1 : item.price_tier_1_qty} kg` : 'Per kg'}: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_1_price}</strong></span>
409	            )}
410	            {item.price_tier_2_qty && item.price_tier_2_price && (
411	              <span>{item.price_tier_2_qty}–{item.price_tier_3_qty ? item.price_tier_3_qty - 1 : '+'} kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_2_price}</strong></span>
412	            )}
413	            {item.price_tier_3_qty && item.price_tier_3_price && (
414	              <span>{item.price_tier_3_qty}+ kg: <strong>₹{item.price_tier_3_price}</strong></span>
415	            )}
416	          </div>
417	        </div>
418	      )}
419	
420	      <div className="px-3 pb-3 pt-2 space-y-2">
421	        <button
422	          onClick={handleAdd}
423	          className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-base transition-colors ${
424	            added
425	              ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
426	              : 'bg-green-700 text-white active:bg-green-800'

442	          >
443	            {L('🗑 Delete', 'తొలగించు')}
444	          </button>
445	        )}
446	      </div>
447	    </div>
448	  )
449	}

451	function ComingSoonCard({ item, farmerId, onDelete }: { item: Produce; farmerId: string; onDelete?: () => void }) {
452	  const { tx, lang, L } = useLang()
453	  const [name, setName] = useState('')
454	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('')
455	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)
456	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
457	
458	  const handleNotify = async () => {
459	    if (!name || !phone) return
460	    setLoading(true)

464	      requester_name: name,

467	    setSubmitted(true)
468	    setLoading(false)
469	  }
470	
471	  return (
472	    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 p-4">
473	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
474	        <div>
475	          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{item.emoji ?? '🌱'} {localizeName(item.name, lang)}</h3>
476	          {item.available_to && (
477	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
478	              {tx.expected} {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
479	            </p>
480	          )}
481	        </div>
482	        <span className="bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
483	          {tx.comingSoon}
484	        </span>
485	      </div>
486	
487	      {submitted ? (
488	        <p className="text-sm text-green-700 font-semibold text-center py-2">{tx.notifySuccess}</p>
489	      ) : (
490	        <div className="space-y-2 mt-3">
491	          <input type="text" placeholder={tx.yourName} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
492	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
493	          <input type="tel" placeholder={tx.yourPhone} value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
494	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm" />
495	          <button onClick={handleNotify} disabled={loading}
496	            className="w-full bg-amber-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-60">
497	            {loading ? tx.saving : tx.notifyMe}
498	          </button>
499	        </div>
500	      )}
501	
502	      {onDelete && (
503	        <button
504	          onClick={onDelete}
505	          className="mt-3 w-full border border-red-200 text-red-600 font-semibold py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm active:bg-red-50"
506	        >
507	          {L('🗑 Delete', 'తొలగించు')}
508	        </button>
509	      )}
510	    </div>
511	  )
512	}
```

```
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
import StoryTab from './tabs/StoryTab'
import ProduceTab from './tabs/ProduceTab'
import QualityTab from './tabs/QualityTab'
import ReviewsTab from './tabs/ReviewsTab'
import FarmMediaTab from './tabs/FarmMediaTab'
import { CartFab } from '@/components/consumer/Cart'

type Props = {
  farmer: Record<string, unknown>
  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
  reviews: Record<string, unknown>[]
  media: Record<string, unknown>[]
}

export default function TabSection({ farmer, produce, reviews, media }: Props) {
  const { tx, L } = useLang()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(1)
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  const isEditMode = searchParams.get('edit') === 'true'

  const TABS = [tx.story, tx.produce, tx.quality, tx.reviews, tx.farm]

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="sticky top-[53px] z-40 bg-white border-b border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
          {TABS.map((tab, i) => (
            <button
              key={tab}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(i)}
              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold border-b-2 transition-colors ${
                activeTab === i
                  ? 'border-green-700 text-green-700'
                  : 'border-transparent text-gray-500'
              }`}
            >
              {tab}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="px-4 py-4">
        {activeTab === 0 && <StoryTab farmer={farmer} />}
        {activeTab === 1 && <ProduceTab farmer={farmer} produce={produce} isEditMode={isEditMode} />}
        {activeTab === 2 && <QualityTab farmer={farmer} produce={produce} />}
        {activeTab === 3 && <ReviewsTab reviews={reviews} farmerId={farmer.id as string} />}
        {activeTab === 4 && <FarmMediaTab media={media} />}
      </div>
      <CartFab />
    </div>
  )
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/farmer/[slug]/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "  // Both sources, newest first — one combined list for the Reviews tab.\n  const allReviews = [...(reviews ?? []), ...mappedProduceReviews].sort(\n    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at as string).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at as string).getTime(),\n  )",
  "new_string": "  // Both sources, newest first — one combined list for the Reviews tab.\n  const allReviews = [...(reviews ?? []), ...mappedProduceReviews].sort(\n    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at as string).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at as string).getTime(),\n  )\n\n  // Per-listing rating summary so the Produce tab can show each produce's own\n  // verified buyer feedback (avg stars, count, latest snippet) on its card —\n  // produceReviews is already ordered newest-first, so the first hit per listing\n  // is the latest review.\n  const reviewAcc = new Map<string, { sum: number; count: number; latest: { name: string; text: string | null; stars: number } | null }>()\n  for (const r of produceReviews ?? []) {\n    if (!r.produce_listing_id) continue\n    const cur = reviewAcc.get(r.produce_listing_id) ?? { sum: 0, count: 0, latest: null }\n    cur.sum += (r.star_rating as number) ?? 0\n    cur.count += 1\n    if (!cur.latest) cur.latest = { name: r.reviewer_name, text: r.review_text, stars: (r.star_rating as number) ?? 0 }\n    reviewAcc.set(r.produce_listing_id, cur)\n  }\n  const produceReviewSummary: Record<string, { avg: number; count: number; latest: { name: string; text: string | null; stars: number } | null }> = {}\n  reviewAcc.forEach((v, k) => {\n    produceReviewSummary[k] = { avg: Math.round((v.sum / v.count) * 10) / 10, count: v.count, latest: v.latest }\n  })"
}
```

```
Effects are intended to synchronize state between React and external systems such as manually updating the DOM, state management libraries, or other platform APIs. In general, the body of an effect should do one or both of the following:
* Update external systems with the latest state from React.
* Subscribe for updates from some external system, calling setState in a callback function when external state changes.

Calling setState synchronously within an effect body causes cascading renders that can hurt performance, and is not recommended. (https://react.dev/learn/you-might-not-need-an-effect).
```

> AGENT

Here's the **"Raise an Intent" form (card #6)** — fields it collects:

| Field | Required | Stored as | How it's used |
|---|---|---|---|
| Crop name | ✅ | `crop_name` | What the buyer wants |
| Quantity (kg) | optional | `quantity_kg` | — |
| Needed-by date | optional | `needed_by_date` | — |
| **Delivery location** | optional | `delivery_location` | Where buyer wants it — but **region is already auto-captured** from the consumer's saved location (`region_slug`), so this is partly redundant |
| Your name | ✅ | `requester_name` | — |
| **WhatsApp number** | ✅ | `requester_phone` | **The only contact channel** — it's how the farmer reaches the buyer when the crop is available ("they'll reach out when available") |

**My read on the client's question:** WhatsApp is essential — without it a raised intent is unactionable (the farmer has no way to contact the buyer). Delivery location is the trimmable one: it's optional and overlaps with the auto-captured region. I'd **keep WhatsApp, drop "Delivery location"** to shorten the form. Want me to remove it?

```
826	  }
827	
828	  const handlePickFile = async (
829	    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
830	    setFile: (f: File | null) => void,
831	    setPreview: (s: string) => void,
832	    currentPreview: string,
833	  ) => {
834	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
835	    if (!file) return
836	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }
837	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }
838	    setError('')
839	    if (currentPreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentPreview)
840	    const compressed = await compressImage(file)
841	    setFile(compressed)
842	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(compressed))
843	  }
844	
845	  const uploadProfileImage = async (file: File, pathSuffix: string): Promise<{ url: string | null; err: string | null }> => {
846	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
847	    // Include timestamp in path so each upload gets a unique URL, busting browser cache
848	    const path = `${farmer.id}/${pathSuffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`
849	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
850	      .from('farm-images')
851	      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
852	    if (upErr) return { url: null, err: `Upload failed: ${upErr.message}` }
853	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
854	    return { url: data.publicUrl, err: null }
855	  }
856
```

```
110	async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
111	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
112	    const img = new Image()
113	    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
114	    img.onload = () => {
115	      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
116	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
117	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
118	      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
119	      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
120	      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
121	      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
122	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
123	      canvas.toBlob(
124	        (blob) => {
125	          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }
126	          const name = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')
127	          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))
128	        },
129	        'image/jpeg',
130	        quality,
131	      )
132	    }
133	    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }
134	    img.src = blobUrl
135	  })
136	}
137
```

## A. Order / Payment detail cards

### ✔️ #1 — Payment showing "UPI – Pending" should be only "UPI"
**Finding:** This was *already fixed* before this session. I searched every farmer-facing surface for a raw `method + status` concatenation and found none.
- In the farmer **order detail page** (`src/app/farmer/dashboard/orders/[id]/page.tsx`), payment is rendered by a `paymentText()` function that returns contextual labels — `"UPI — Paid"`, `"UPI — Buyer paid, verify"`, or `"Payment Pending"` — never the literal `"UPI - Pending"`.
- In the farmer **OrderCard** (`src/components/farmer/OrderCard.tsx`), the badge shows `"📲 UPI"` when unpaid — just "UPI", as requested.
- The gateway name "razorpay" is deliberately never shown to farmers; it's mapped to "UPI".

**Conclusion:** Nothing to change. This is one of the "already done" cards.

### ✔️ #2 — Farmer order details should show self-pickup date
**Finding:** Already present. The order detail page shows the pickup/delivery date in two places:
- A labeled field in the summary grid (`Pickup date` / `Delivery date`) showing `fulfillment_date`, or "Not set" if empty.
- Again inside the fulfilment card (`📅 Pickup date: …`).

The label switches automatically between "Pickup" and "Delivery" based on `delivery_type`. Nothing to change.

### ✔️ #3 — Show the date of each status in the progress picture
**Finding:** Already present. The order detail page builds a `milestones[]` timeline (Order placed → Approved → Shipped → Picked up/Received). Each milestone carries an `at` timestamp, and the render shows it: `{m.at && m.done && <p>{fmt(m.at)}</p>}` — so each completed status displays its date underneath. Nothing to change.

## B. Produce, search & profile cards

### ✅ #4 — Produce review missing in the Produce tab
**What was wrong:** Verified buyer feedback (the ⭐ button buyers tap on orders) lives in the `produce_reviews` table and was already shown in the **Reviews tab**, but **not** on each produce in the **Produce tab**.

**What I did (3 files):**
1. `src/app/farmer/[slug]/page.tsx` — the page already fetched `produce_reviews`. I added an aggregation that builds a **per-listing summary**: for each produce, its average stars, review count, and the latest review (name + text + stars). Because the query is already ordered newest-first, the first hit per listing is the latest review.
2. `src/components/farmer/TabSection.tsx` — added a `produceReviews` prop (typed `ProduceReviewSummary`) and passed it down to `ProduceTab`.
3. `src/components/farmer/tabs/ProduceTab.tsx` — each `ProduceCard` now receives its `review` summary and renders:
   - A **⭐ rating pill** (`⭐ 4.5 (12)`) in the badges row.
   - A **latest-review snippet** below the description (`★★★★★ "great tomatoes" — Ravi`).

No schema change needed — `produce_reviews` already exists.

### ✅ #8 — Search has 3 methods but farmers can pick 4 + rename "Low Chemical → Semi Organic"
**Two parts:**
1. **Add the missing 4th method to search.** The consumer search filter (`src/app/consumer/page.tsx`) only offered Natural / Organic / Low-chemical. I added a **🧪 Chemical** option so it matches what farmers can actually select when adding produce.
2. **Rename "Low Chemical" → "Semi Organic"** everywhere it's user-visible. Important detail: I changed **display labels only** — the underlying database value stays `low_chemical`, so existing data and filters keep working. Files touched:
   - `src/lib/translations.ts` — `methodLowChemical` and `lowChemicalMethod` → "Semi Organic" (this drives the farmer add-produce form label).
   - `src/app/consumer/page.tsx` — the short pill label (`Low-chem` → `Semi-org`) and the search dropdown option.
   - Moderator surfaces for consistency: `moderator/listings/page.tsx`, `moderator/my-farmers/page.tsx`, `moderator/farmers/page.tsx`, `moderator/listings/new/page.tsx`, and `components/moderator/ModeratorFarmerForm.tsx`.

**What was wrong:** The "Approved this week" tile was a dead, non-clickable stat box.
**What I did:** Made it a link to `/farmer/dashboard/orders?status=approved&time=all`. I first verified the Orders page actually reads those query params (it does — it whitelists `status` and `time` values), so the deep link lands on the correctly-filtered list.

### ✅ #15 — Remove the "Today's Schedule" section
**This was the biggest/most delicate change.** The "Today's Schedule" block wasn't just a list — it was the dashboard's **entire inline order-management engine**: approve, decline, set date, mark paid, update payment, mark shipped, acknowledge cancellation, plus a decline-reason sheet and a decline-success sheet.

**Why it's safe to remove:** All of that same functionality already exists on the full Orders page, which the tiles now deep-link into. So removing it from the dashboard loses no capability — it just declutters, exactly as the card intends.

**How I removed it cleanly (one coordinated excision in `dashboard/page.tsx`):**
- The `<TodayScheduleSection>` render block.
- The two decline sheets (`DeclineReasonSheet`, `DeclineSuccessSheet` renders) — now unreachable.
- **7 handler functions** used only by that section: `handleSetFulfillmentDate`, `handleApprove`, `handleAcknowledgeCancel`, `handleConfirmDecline`, `handleMarkPaid`, `handleUpdatePaymentStatus`, `handleMarkShipped`.
- The state they used: `processingOrderId`, `processingPaidId`, `decliningOrder`, `declineResult`.
- The `TodayScheduleSection` component definition itself (~90 lines).
- The now-unused `DeclineSheets` import and the unused default `OrderCard` import (kept the named `Order` type + `isResolved` which are still used).

Before cutting, I confirmed via grep that **none of those handlers/state were referenced anywhere else**, so nothing else broke. The file went from 3,223 → ~2,960 lines. tsc and the production build both pass.

## E. Farmer profile data cards (the big investigation)

These four turned out to be related, and the key insight is that the **display already existed** — the gap was **data entry**.

### ✅ #17 — Profile missing WhatsApp, Farm Size, Soil Organic Carbon, Story/Quote
### ✅ #19 — Soil pH missing
**What I found:**
- The public profile **already displays** these — `StoryTab` shows story/quote, farm size, phone; `QualityTab` shows soil organic carbon and soil pH.
- The columns **already exist** in the `farmers` table (`story_quote`, `farm_size_acres`, `soil_organic_carbon`, `phone`) — confirmed by the moderator farmer form/API which read and write them. The moderator form even labels phone as **"Phone (WhatsApp)"**, confirming phone *is* the WhatsApp number (no separate column).
- The real problem: the **farmer dashboard had no editor** for them, so they showed up blank. Soil **pH** was edited *nowhere at all* (not even moderator) — that's the specific #19 gap.

**What I did:** Extended the existing `ProfileEditModal` in the dashboard:
1. Added the `Farmer` type fields (`farm_size_acres`, `soil_organic_carbon`, `soil_ph`, `story_quote`).
2. Added form state and inputs: **WhatsApp number**, **Farm size (acres)**, **Soil organic carbon (%)**, **Soil pH**, and a **Story/Quote** textarea — placed right after "Farming since".
3. Wired them into the save handler. WhatsApp, farm size, soil carbon, and story save in the main `farmers` update.
4. **Soil pH is saved "best-effort"** in a separate follow-up update, because its column may not exist yet — so a missing column can never block the rest of the profile save. I merge the pH value into the returned object so the UI updates immediately.

**Migration I created:** `scripts/farmer-soil-ph-migration.sql` →
```sql
ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS soil_ph numeric;
```
**This is the one thing pending on your side** — pH won't persist until you run this in Supabase. Everything else persists today.

### 🟡 #18 — Where is "Soil Health / How we grow" entered?
**Status: answered.** The soil-health card in the Quality tab is **derived** from the `soil_organic_carbon`, `soil_ph`, and `method` columns. Those were historically entered via the **moderator farmer form**, and now **also** via the farmer dashboard editor (from my #17/#19 work). There's no separate free-text "how we grow" column — the `story_quote` field plays that role. No code change needed beyond what #17/#19 already added.

### 🟡 #20 — Chemicals & Buyers showing zero
**Status: root-caused, needs your product decision.**
- **"Chemicals"** is literally **hardcoded to `'0'`** in `src/components/farmer/TrustStrip.tsx` (line 25) — it ignores the farmer entirely. So it'll always read 0.
- **"Buyers"** reads the real column `farmers.buyer_count`, but **nothing in the codebase increments it**, so it stays 0.

I didn't guess a fix because the right behavior is a product call:
- Should "Chemicals: 0" remain a fixed trust badge (meaning "no chemicals used"), reflect the farmer's actual method, or be removed?
- Should `buyer_count` be computed from real completed orders (and if so, distinct buyers all-time)?

**Not changed — awaiting your direction.**

Here's the precise picture — and it explains your question directly.

## Today: there is **no** option for consumers to manage their intents

Right now `demand_intents` only supports the **"C"** of CRUD for consumers:

| Operation | Who can do it today | Where |
|---|---|---|
| **Create** | ✅ Consumer | The "Raise Intent" banner on the home page (`consumer/page.tsx` → inserts into `demand_intents`) |
| **Read** | ❌ Consumer — only farmers (aggregate demand) & moderators see them | farmer dashboard, moderator supply/consumers/audit |
| **Update** | ❌ Consumer — only **moderators** | `/api/moderator/demand-intents/[id]` |
| **Delete** | ❌ Consumer — only **moderators** | same moderator route |

So to answer literally: **there is no "raised intents" option in the consumer's area at all yet.** That's exactly what **card #7** is — and it's still in the *"approved, not yet built"* bucket. I haven't created it.

## Two things make this more than a simple add

**1. There's no consumer "profile" page to put it in.** The consumer area only has these pages:
- `/consumer` (home), `/consumer/orders`, `/consumer/orders/history`, `/consumer/cart`, `/consumer/complaints`

There's no account/profile screen. So the "My Intents" view needs a home — the natural spot is **next to "My Orders"** (e.g., a new `/consumer/intents` page, linked from the home banner and/or near the My Orders chip).

**2. Raised intents aren't linked to a consumer account.** Today an intent stores only `requester_name` and `requester_phone` — there's no `consumer_id`. So to show "*your* intents," I have to identify ownership. Let me confirm what identity the logged-in consumer has:

> AGENT

Confirmed: the logged-in consumer is identified by `{ id, name, phone }`. So I now have everything needed to design **#7**.

## Direct answer to your question
**That option doesn't exist anywhere yet** — not in a profile, not on the home page, nowhere. A consumer can only *raise* an intent. Seeing/editing/deleting their own intents is **card #7, which I haven't built** (you approved it, but it's still pending). So you're not missing a hidden screen — it genuinely isn't there.

## How I'd build #7 (and where the option would live)

**Ownership — the one real decision.** Right now an intent stores only `requester_name` + `requester_phone`, with **no link to the consumer account**. Two ways to fix:

| Option | How | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| **A — Add `consumer_id` (recommended)** | New column on `demand_intents`; stamp the logged-in consumer's id when they raise an intent; "My Intents" filters on it | Rock-solid ownership; needs a 1-line migration |
| **B — Match by phone** | List intents where `requester_phone == account phone` | No migration, but breaks if they typed a *different* WhatsApp number than their login phone (the form allows that today) |

I'd go with **A** — it's the correct model and avoids the phone-mismatch hole.

**Then the actual feature:**
1. **New page `/consumer/intents`** — a list of the consumer's raised intents, each showing crop, qty, date, status (pending/fulfilled), with **Edit** and **Remove** actions.
2. **Entry point** — placed **right next to "My Orders"** (the `MyOrdersChip` row / home banner), since there's no profile page. So the home banner's "Raise Intent" gets a companion "My Intents" link.
3. **Stamp ownership on create** — update the existing Raise-Intent form to record `consumer_id` (and pre-fill their account phone).
4. **Edit/Delete** — guarded so a consumer can only touch their own rows.

This pairs naturally with **#6** (I'd also pre-fill/trim that same form while I'm in it).

Want me to go ahead and build **#7 with Option A** (add `consumer_id` + new `/consumer/intents` page + entry point next to My Orders)? I'll write the migration file alongside it, the same way I did for soil pH.

[]) as Intent[])\n    setLoading(false)\n  }, [consumer])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (state.status === 'authenticated') void refresh()\n    else if (state.status === 'anonymous') setLoading(false)\n  }, [state.status, refresh])\n\n  const startEdit = (it: Intent) => {\n    setEditingId(it.id)\n    setDraft({\n      crop: it.crop_name ?? ## ✅ Card #7 built — consumer intent CRUD

Yes, the card requires it and now it exists. Here's exactly what I added (build-verified):

**1. New page `/consumer/intents`** — the consumer's own intents list:
- **Read** — shows each intent: crop, quantity, needed-by date, location, and an **Open / Fulfilled** status badge.
- **Update** — an **✎ Edit** button opens inline fields (crop, qty, date, location) → Save. (Edit is hidden once an intent is *fulfilled*, since a farmer already acted on it.)
- **Delete** — a **🗑 Remove** button with a confirm.
- Proper auth gate: logged-out users get a "Log in" prompt; loading and empty states handled.

**2. Where the option lives** (your original question): on the home page's "Raise Intent" banner —
- a **"📋 My raised intents"** button appears under "Raise Intent" when logged in, and
- a **"View my intents →"** link shows right after you submit a new one.

(There's no consumer profile page in the app, so the banner next to Raise/My Orders is the natural home.)

**3. Ownership wiring (Option A):** raising an intent now stamps your `consumer_id`, and the form pre-fills your account name/phone. The list/edit/delete all filter by `consumer_id`, so you only ever see and touch your own.

## ⚠️ Two things to know
1. **Apply the migration first:** `scripts/consumer-intents-migration.sql` (adds `consumer_id` + index). **Until you run it in Supabase, the My Intents page will error** (it queries a column that doesn't exist yet). This is the one blocker for the feature to work.
2. **Old intents** raised before this won't have a `consumer_id`, so they won't appear in anyone's list — only intents raised from now on are attributed. (Expected with Option A.)

## Where things stand now
- **Migrations waiting on you (2):** `farmer-soil-ph-migration.sql` (#19) and `consumer-intents-migration.sql` (#7).
- **Still to do:** **#12** (multiple produce photos — approved), and decisions on **#6** (remove delivery location?) and **#20** (chemicals/buyers stat).

Want me to move on to **#12** next, and should I action **#6** while I'm in that same intent form?

## The missed one — #9 (category when adding produce)

I'd only *flagged* this earlier; it was never in an approved batch, so **no code was written for it.** Here's the actual situation I just confirmed:

- The consumer's category chips (Vegetables / Fruits / Grains & Pulses / Leafy Greens) filter through `/api/produce/search`, which uses **`CATEGORY_KEYWORDS`** — it guesses a produce's category by **matching its name** against keyword lists.
- There is **no `category` column** on `produce_listings`, and **no category selector** in either add-produce form (the dashboard `ProduceListingForm` or the profile `AddProduceForm`).
- So the client is right: a farmer adding produce sees no property to pick its category — categorization is implicit/name-based, which is fragile (a misspelled or unusual crop name lands in the wrong bucket or none).

**The fix** would be: add a category dropdown to the add-produce form + a `category` column (migration), and have search prefer the explicit category over keyword-guessing. That's a clean, self-contained task.

## So, did we miss any?
**Just #9** as a true oversight. The rest are accounted for: 11 implemented (4,7,8,10,11,14,15,16,17,19 + your #5), 3 already-done (1,2,3), 1 declined (13), 1 answer-only (18), and 3 intentionally pending (6, 12, 20).

```
=== #20: TrustStrip stats exact ===
'use client'

import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'

type Farmer = {
  farming_since_year: number
  rating_avg: number
  buyer_count: number
}

export default function TrustStrip({
  farmer,
  produceCount,
}: {
  farmer: Farmer
  produceCount: number
}) {
  const { tx, L } = useLang()
  const yearsfarming = new Date().getFullYear() - farmer.farming_since_year

  const stats = [
    { label: tx.yearsFarming, value: yearsfarming.toString() },
    { label: tx.starRating, value: `${farmer.rating_avg} ★` },
    { label: tx.buyers, value: farmer.buyer_count.toString() },
    { label: tx.chemicals, value: '0' },
    { label: tx.produceNow, value: `${produceCount}` },
  ]

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-100">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-5 divide-x divide-gray-100">
        {stats.map((stat) => (
          <div key={stat.label} className="flex flex-col items-center py-3 px-1">
            <span className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900">{stat.value}</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 text-center leading-tight mt-0.5">
              {stat.label}
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

> AGENT

Here's the detailed, code-verified breakdown of those three — and #18 has an important catch I want to be straight about.

## #18 — "Where is Soil Health / How we grow entered?"

This is the card asking where the content on a farmer's profile (e.g. Kapil Konedena's) actually comes from. The honest answer, after reading `QualityTab.tsx` + `translations.ts`, is **it's a mix — and a lot of it is hardcoded, entered nowhere per-farmer:**

| Thing shown on profile | Source | Editable? |
|---|---|---|
| **Soil Health → Organic Carbon** | `farmers.soil_organic_carbon` column | ✅ moderator form + now farmer editor |
| **Soil Health → Soil pH** | `farmers.soil_ph` column | ✅ now (via #19) |
| **Soil Health → NPK Balance** | **Hardcoded** to "Natural" (`tx.natural`, always green) | ❌ not data at all |
| **"Last soil test: ANGRAU Lab, Guntur"** | **Hardcoded string** in `QualityTab.tsx` | ❌ same text for every farmer |
| **"How we grow" paragraph** | **Hardcoded** `tx.howWeGrowDesc` in `translations.ts` | ❌ **identical generic text for every farmer** |

So the client's confusion is justified: the **"How we grow" description, NPK, and the soil-test lab line are static placeholder content** — the *same* for every farmer, not entered anywhere. Only the two soil numbers are real per-farmer data.

**My status earlier ("answered, no code") was only half the story.** What's actually true: the soil *numbers* are now enterable, but "How we grow" / NPK / lab are still generic hardcoded text. If you want those to be real per-farmer, that's a small follow-up (a `how_we_grow` text column + an editor field — I can reuse the same profile editor I just extended). **Right now: not done as real data.**

## #19 — Soil pH ✅ Done (one migration pending)

- The profile **already displayed** `soil_ph` in the Soil Health section (showing "—" when empty).
- The gap was: **nothing let anyone enter it** — not the farmer dashboard, not even the moderator form.
- **What I did:** added a **Soil pH** input to the farmer dashboard profile editor, saved best-effort so it never blocks the rest of the save.
- **Pending on you:** run `scripts/farmer-soil-ph-migration.sql` (`ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN soil_ph numeric`). Until then, pH won't persist (but nothing errors).

This one is genuinely finished code-wise.

## #20 — Chemicals & Buyers showing 0 🟡 Root-caused, not changed (needs your decision)

I confirmed the exact source in `TrustStrip.tsx`:

```js
{ label: tx.buyers,    value: farmer.buyer_count.toString() },  // real column…
{ label: tx.chemicals, value: '0' },                            // …but this is HARDCODED
```

- **"Chemicals: 0"** → literally the string `'0'`, ignores the farmer entirely. Even a chemical-method farmer would show 0.
- **"Buyers"** → reads the real `farmers.buyer_count` column, but **nothing in the codebase ever increments it**, so it stays 0 for everyone.

I deliberately didn't "fix" these because the right behavior is a product call:

**For Chemicals** — pick one:
1. Keep it as a fixed trust badge meaning "0 chemicals used" (rename label to make intent clear).
2. Make it reflect the farmer's method (Natural/Organic → 0; Semi-Organic/Chemical → show accordingly).
3. Remove the stat.

**For Buyers** — should `buyer_count` be computed from real completed orders (distinct buyers)? If yes, I'd either compute it on read from the `orders` table, or maintain it via a DB trigger/periodic job.

## Summary of the three
- **#19** — ✅ done (apply the pH migration).
- **#18** — ⚠️ partially: soil numbers are real & now editable, but "How we grow"/NPK/soil-test are still **hardcoded generic text** for every farmer — needs a small follow-up if you want them real.
- **#20** — 🟡 root-caused, **not changed**; needs your decision on what Chemicals/Buyers should actually show.

Want me to (a) make "How we grow" a real editable field, (b) implement a choice for Chemicals/Buyers, or both? Tell me the behavior you want for #20 and I'll wire it.

```
      const editPayload: Record<string, unknown> = {
        name: name.trim(),
        emoji,
        method: farmingMethod,
        unit,
        variety: variety.trim() || null,
        stock_qty: qty ? Number(qty) : null,
        description: description.trim() || null,
        brix: brix ? Number(brix) : null,
        soil_organic_carbon: soc ? Number(soc) : null,
        price_tier_1_price: price1 ? Number(price1) : null,
        price_tier_1_qty: price1 ? Number(price1Qty) : null,
        price_tier_2_price: price2 ? Number(price2) : null,
        price_tier_2_qty: price2 ? Number(price2Qty) : null,
        price_tier_3_price: price3 ? Number(price3) : null,
        price_tier_3_qty: price3 ? Number(Number(price2Qty) + 1) : null,
        image_url: imageUrl,
        harvest_date: harvestDate || null,
        availability_period: period.trim() || null,
        delivery_mode: deliveryMode,
        delivery_charge: deliveryMode === 'pickup' ? null : (deliveryCharge ? Number(deliveryCharge) : null),
...
      emoji,
      method: farmingMethod,
      unit,
    }
    if (variety.trim()) payload.variety = variety.trim()
    if (qty) payload.stock_qty = Number(qty)
    if (description.trim()) payload.description = description.trim()
    else payload.description = null
    if (brix) payload.brix = Number(brix)
    if (soc) payload.soil_organic_carbon = Number(soc)
    if (price1) { payload.price_tier_1_price = Number(price1); payload.price_tier_1_qty = Number(price1Qty) }
    if (price2) { payload.price_tier_2_price = Number(price2); payload.price_tier_2_qty = Number(price2Qty) }
    if (price3) { payload.price_tier_3_price = Number(price3); payload.price_tier_3_qty = Number(price2Qty) + 1 }
    payload.image_url = imageUrl
    payload.harvest_date = harvestDate || null
    payload.availability_period = period.trim() || null
```

Done — build-verified. Here's what I added so the farmer enters the #18/#19/#20 info **per produce, in the add-produce form**.

## What's now in the add-produce form
Three new fields, alongside the existing BRIX / Soil Organic Carbon:
- **Soil pH** — number input, **pre-filled from the farm profile** (the `soil_ph` you set in the profile editor) but **overridable** for that specific produce.
- **Chemicals / pesticide** — text (e.g. "None" / "Lab tested"). This reuses the existing `pesticide_result` column.
- **How we grow** — a growing-notes textarea (so each produce gets its *own* real notes instead of the generic hardcoded "How we grow" text).

This works in **all three** entry points: the main "Add produce" form, and the **edit** + **add** forms inside the Manage Listings modal.

## Where buyers see it
On the farmer's public profile → **Produce tab**, each produce card now shows:
- a **pH** badge (purple), the **chemicals/pesticide** badge (existing blue), and
- a **"🌱 How we grow"** notes block under the description.

## Safety built in (important)
The three columns may not exist until you run the migration, so I made all of this **migration-tolerant**:
- The fields are saved **best-effort** (a secondary update after the listing is created/edited), so **adding or editing produce never breaks** even before the migration runs.
- The listings load now uses `select('*')` so it won't error on missing columns.
- **Migration to apply:** `scripts/produce-quality-fields-migration.sql` (adds `soil_ph`, `how_we_grow`, `pesticide_result` to `produce_listings`). Until you run it, the fields just won't persist — but nothing errors.

## Quick notes
- I added the three fields you picked (Soil pH, Chemicals/pesticide, How-we-grow) — **not** soil organic carbon (it was already there as a field anyway).
- This is the **per-produce** version. The farm-level **#20 TrustStrip** stat (Chemicals hardcoded `'0'`, Buyers `buyer_count` never incremented) is a *separate* surface and still needs your decision — want me to handle that too?

```
  const doSearch = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
      setFiltered(available)
      return
    }
    searchAbortRef.current?.abort()
    searchAbortRef.current = new AbortController()
    try {
      const p = new URLSearchParams()
      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`, { cache: 'no-store', signal: searchAbortRef.current.signal })
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
    } catch (e) {
      if ((e as Error).name !== 'AbortError') { /* silent */ }
    }
  }, [search, method, category, available])

=== #9: search route CATEGORY_KEYWORDS + category filter ===
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
export const fetchCache = 'force-no-store'

const CATEGORY_KEYWORDS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  vegetables: [
    'tomato', 'capsicum', 'brinjal', 'eggplant', 'bean', 'pea', 'pumpkin',
    'gourd', 'onion', 'potato', 'garlic', 'carrot', 'radish', 'cucumber',
    'okra', 'ladyfinger', 'drumstick', 'ridge gourd', 'bottle gourd', 'chilli',
    'pepper', 'bitter gourd', 'cluster bean', 'snake gourd',
  ],
  fruits: [
    'mango', 'banana', 'papaya', 'guava', 'pomegranate', 'orange', 'lime',
    'lemon', 'coconut', 'tamarind', 'jackfruit', 'sapota', 'fig', 'grapes',
    'watermelon', 'muskmelon', 'pineapple', 'custard apple',
  ],
  grains: [
    'rice', 'wheat', 'jowar', 'bajra', 'ragi', 'maize', 'corn', 'dal',
    'lentil', 'chickpea', 'groundnut', 'sesame', 'turmeric', 'ginger',
    'pulses', 'toor', 'moong', 'urad', 'chana',
  ],
  leafy: [
    'spinach', 'methi', 'fenugreek', 'coriander', 'mint', 'curry',
    'amaranth', 'sorrel', 'moringa', 'drumstick leaves', 'palak',
  ],
}

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
  const method = searchParams.get('method') ?? ''
  const category = searchParams.get('category') ?? ''

  const { data: produce } = await supabase
    .from('produce_listings')
    .select('*')
    .eq('status', 'available')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })

  let filtered = produce ?? []

  if (q) {
    const query = q.toLowerCase()
    filtered = filtered.filter(
      (p) =>
        p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        p.variety?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        p.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    )
  }

  if (method && method !== 'all') {
    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
      p.method?.toLowerCase().includes(method.toLowerCase())
    )
  }

  if (category && category !== 'all' && CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]) {
    const keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]
    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
      keywords.some((kw) => p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
    )
  }

  if (!filtered.length) return NextResponse.json([])

  const farmerIds = [...new Set(filtered.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]

  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
    .from('farmers')
    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, pickup_locations, pickup_slots')
    .in('id', farmerIds)
    .eq('active', true)
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx",
  "old_string": "  const uploadImage = async (file: File): Promise<string | null> => {\n    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n    const path = `${farmerId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}.${ext}`\n    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n      .from('farm-images')\n      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })\n    if (upErr) {\n      setError(`Image upload failed: ${upErr.message}`)\n      return null\n    }\n    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)\n    return data.publicUrl\n  }",
  "new_string": "  const uploadImage = async (file: File): Promise<string | null> => {\n    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n    const path = `${farmerId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}.${ext}`\n    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n      .from('farm-images')\n      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })\n    if (upErr) {\n      setError(`Image upload failed: ${upErr.message}`)\n      return null\n    }\n    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)\n    return data.publicUrl\n  }\n\n  // #12 — add/remove extra produce photos (beyond the cover).\n  const handlePickExtra = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n    if (!file) return\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }\n    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }\n    setError('')\n    const compressed = await compressImage(file)\n    setExtraFiles((f) => [...f, compressed])\n    setExtraPreviews((p) => [...p, URL.createObjectURL(compressed)])\n    e.target.value = '' // allow picking the same file again\n  }\n  const removeNewExtra = (idx: number) => {\n    setExtraPreviews((p) => { if (p[idx]) URL.revokeObjectURL(p[idx]); return p.filter((_, i) => i !== idx) })\n    setExtraFiles((f) => f.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))\n  }\n  const removeExistingExtra = (url: string) =>\n    setExistingExtraUrls((p) => p.filter((u) => u !== url))"
}
```

> AGENT

Both done and build-verified. Here's what shipped.

## ✅ #9 — Category when adding produce
- **New "Category" dropdown** in the add-produce form: Vegetables / Fruits / Grains & Pulses / Leafy Greens (values match the consumer filter chips exactly).
- **Search now uses it:** `/api/produce/search` prefers the farmer's explicit `category`, and only falls back to the old name-keyword guessing for older listings that don't have one set. So filtering becomes reliable instead of guess-based.
- Saved **best-effort** (migration-tolerant) — adding produce never breaks if the column isn't there yet.

## ✅ #12 — Multiple produce photos
- **Cover photo** stays the existing single picker (so every existing display keeps working).
- **New "More photos" section** below it: add as many extra photos as you want, each shown as a thumbnail with a × to remove (works for both newly-picked and already-saved photos).
- All photos are stored in a new `image_urls` array (cover first), saved **best-effort**.
- **Consumer view:** on the farmer's profile → Produce tab, the cover now shows a **📷 N** badge when there are multiple, and **tapping it opens a full-screen lightbox** you swipe through. Tap anywhere / the × to close.

## 📦 Migrations — now **4 files / 8 statements**
The new one adds 2 columns. Here's the **complete consolidated block** (run once in Supabase, safe to re-run):

```sql
ALTER TABLE farmers          ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS soil_ph numeric;

ALTER TABLE demand_intents   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS consumer_id uuid;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS demand_intents_consumer_id_idx ON demand_intents (consumer_id);

ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS soil_ph numeric;
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS how_we_grow text;
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_result text;
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS category text;
ALTER TABLE produce_listings ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS image_urls jsonb;
```

## Board status now
The only things left on the whole Trello board:
- **#6** — quick: confirm and I'll remove "Delivery location" from the intent form.
- **#20** — needs your decision on what the farm-level Chemicals/Buyers stat should show.
- *(optional #18)* — clean up the hardcoded farm-level "How we grow"/NPK/soil-test text.

Want me to action **#6** now and take a direction on **#20**?

> DEVELOPER

is there very importatnt questions or doubts to ask for clinet

> AGENT

Yes — a handful of genuinely important ones. I'd separate them into "must ask" (trust/correctness — don't guess) and "should ask" (product choices), plus a couple of operational ones. Here's what I'd take to the client.

## 🔴 Must ask — trust / correctness (don't guess these)

**1. The hardcoded profile text (#18) — potentially misleading to buyers.**
Right now **every farmer's profile shows the *same* fixed text**, presented as if it's their real data:
- *"Last soil test: ANGRAU Lab, Guntur"*
- *"We follow traditional natural farming… no chemical fertilizers, no pesticides, no hybrid seeds."*
- *"NPK Balance: Natural"*

On a trust-based marketplace, showing identical lab-test + farming claims for farmers who may not all do this is a real credibility/liability risk.
**Ask:** Remove it, make it real per-farmer (editable), or explicitly keep as generic marketing copy?

**2. The "Chemicals" and "Buyers" stats (#20) — currently fake/zero.**
- "Chemicals" is **hardcoded to 0** for everyone (even a chemical-method farmer shows 0).
- "Buyers" reads a counter that **nothing ever updates**, so it's 0 for all.
These are public trust numbers.
**Ask:** What should each actually show? (e.g. Buyers = count of real distinct buyers from completed orders; Chemicals = derived from method, or remove it.)

## 🟠 Should ask — product decisions

**3. Existing produce has no category (#9).**
New listings get a category, but **all current listings don't** — so the Vegetables/Fruits/etc. filter still relies on name-guessing for them until each is re-saved.
**Ask:** Should we do a one-time backfill (or have moderators set categories for existing produce)?

**4. Should raising an intent require login (#7)?**
Today anyone can raise an intent without logging in — but those **won't appear in their "My Intents"** (nothing to attribute them to). Also, old intents from before this change are orphaned.
**Ask:** Require login to raise an intent (so it's always manageable)? And is consumer self-edit/delete of intents acceptable security-wise (needs DB-level row protection)?

**5. Two sources for soil pH now (profile vs per-produce).**
A farmer can set soil pH on their **profile** and **per produce**. The profile's Quality tab still shows the farm-level one.
**Ask:** Which is the source of truth on the profile — keep farm-level, or show it from the produce?

**6. Delivery location on "Raise Intent" (#6).**
Minor: it's optional and overlaps with the auto-captured region.
**Ask:** Remove it to shorten the form? (My recommendation: yes.)

## ⚙️ Operational (for whoever deploys, not really the client)
- **Migrations must be applied** (the 8 SQL statements) — until then new values silently don't save and the **My Intents page errors**. Coordinate migration + deploy together.
- **Photos (#12):** any **max number** of photos per produce, or storage-cost concern on slow 4G? (Currently unlimited.)
- **Language:** your rule says English-only MVP, but the code still carries Telugu strings (i18n toggle mid-migration per your notes). Confirm new UI text should stay English-only.
